It is accusative as the object reached by {{ar:نَأْتِى}} ({{tr:naʾtī}}), then it is picked up by the suffixes in {{ar:نَنقُصُهَا}} ({{tr:nanquṣuhā}}) and {{ar:أَطْرَافِهَا}} ({{tr:ʾaṭrāfihā}}). The definite singular lets the referent range from recognized territory to the earth-domain, while the edge phrase keeps that range spatial and concrete. The common land noun becomes marked here because it is paired with the rare reduction construction and then handed into the sovereignty clause.\",\"root_display\":\"{{ar:أ ر ض}} ({{tr:ʾ-r-ḍ}})\",\"root_gloss_range\":\"ground, land, territory, and earth-domain; locally concrete through edges and reduction\",\"surface_display\":\"{{ar:ٱلْأَرْضَ}} ({{tr:al-ʾarḍa}})\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"micro","source_local_id":"13:41:12:verb-noun-echo","source_type":"word_analysis","support_id":"sup_114825514f963c0868fa","text":"{\"blocking_evidence\":null,\"headline\":\"verb-to-noun judgment echo\",\"reader_payoff\":\"The reader notices the act of judging echoed as the possessed judgment that cannot be reversed.\",\"reason\":\"The root recurs in {{ar:حُكْمِهِۦ}} ({{tr:ḥukmihī}}), moving from act to judgment-product.\",\"representative_source_ids\":[\"QI-e218af69\",\"QE-0a2e8d69\",\"ME-896a69fc\"],\"status\":\"used\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"micro","source_local_id":"13:41:18","source_type":"word_analysis","support_id":"sup_11d4a497f0be0e233a46","text":"{\"gloss_range\":\"detached pronoun resuming God as subject of the final nominal attribute\",\"prose\":\"{{ar:هُوَ}} ({{tr:huwa}}) resumes the divine subject explicitly for the final attribute. The detached pronoun lets the closure stand as a full nominal clause, presenting swiftness in reckoning as a stable attribute. It also completes the ayah’s person movement from divine We acting in the land to He characterized in judgment and reckoning.\",\"root_display\":\"no lexical root\",\"root_gloss_range\":null,\"surface_display\":\"{{ar:هُوَ}} ({{tr:huwa}})\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"micro","source_local_id":"13:41:5","source_type":"word_analysis","support_id":"sup_13a447739de0b5e9200c","text":"{\"gloss_range\":\"divine coming/approach with the land as object\",\"prose\":\"{{ar:نَأْتِى}} ({{tr:naʾtī}}) gives the divine action its first verb: We come to the land. The definite land is the direct object, so the coming has a target, and the following {{ar:نَنقُصُهَا}} ({{tr:nanquṣuhā}}) explains what that coming looks like: visible diminution. The imperfect first-person form presents the action as an observable process, and across the boundary from 13:40 the reaching of a message gives way to direct divine approach. The broader bringing range of {{ar:أ ت ي}} ({{tr:ʾ-t-y}}) remains a pressure of effective arrival, but local grammar supplies no second object for a delivered thing.\",\"root_display\":\"{{ar:أ ت ي}} ({{tr:ʾ-t-y}})\",\"root_gloss_range\":\"coming, approaching, arriving, and bringing; locally narrowed by one explicit object and the following reduction clause\",\"surface_display\":\"{{ar:نَأْتِى}} ({{tr:naʾtī}})\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"micro","source_local_id":"13:41:12","source_type":"word_analysis","support_id":"sup_15c7864c0736268d26b9","text":"{\"gloss_range\":\"He judges/rules, used absolutely as the verbal predicate of God\",\"prose\":\"{{ar:يَحْكُمُ}} ({{tr:yaḥkumu}}) is the active verbal predicate of the named subject. It takes no explicit object here, so the judgment is stated absolutely rather than limited to a named case. The imperfect third-person form shifts from the first-person action sequence into an ongoing public declaration about God. The root’s judging, governing, and restraining range makes the next clause intelligible: because God judges with ordering force, no follow-up authority can alter His judgment, and the act of judging is echoed by the possessed judgment that cannot be reversed.\",\"root_display\":\"{{ar:ح ك م}} ({{tr:ḥ-k-m}})\",\"root_gloss_range\":\"judging, governing, restraining, and setting in order;
